In this paper, we generate similar allometric scaling laws for bipedal robots across three orders of magnitude in leg length. First, we conduct a review of legged robots from the literature and extract empirical relationships between leg length (L), body length, mass, and speed. These data show that robot mass scales more closely to L^2, in contrast to the L^3 scaling predicted by isometric scaling. We then perform controlled simulation studies in Drake using three variants of real quasi-passive, hip-actuated walkers with different foot geometries and control strategies. We evaluate the performance of each design scaled with leg length, L. Across all robots, walking velocity follows the expected L^(1/2) trend from dynamic similarity. Minimum required torque scales more closely with m*L than the isometric model of m*L^2. Foot geometry scaled proportionally with L^1. These results provide new insight into how robot designs allometrically scale to different sizes, and how that scaling is different from isometric or biological scaling laws.

</details>


### [149] [GIFT: Generalizing Intent for Flexible Test-Time Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22574)
*Fin Amin,Nathaniel Dennler,Andreea Bobu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GIFT框架通过语言模型从用户演示中推断高层意图，基于意图而非表面线索实现奖励函数在分布偏移下的泛化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖视觉或语义相似性来改进奖励泛化，但这些表面线索常与人类实际关注点不符，导致学习到的奖励函数在新环境中失效

Method: GIFT利用语言模型通过对比偏好与非偏好行为从用户演示中推断高层意图；在部署时，通过意图条件相似性将新测试状态映射到行为等效的训练状态，无需重新训练即可实现奖励泛化

Result: 在4个模拟任务和50多个未见物体上，GIFT在测试时配对胜率和状态对齐F1分数上持续优于视觉和语义相似性基线；7自由度Franka Panda机器人的真实世界实验表明GIFT能可靠迁移到物理环境

Conclusion: GIFT通过将奖励泛化建立在人类意图而非表面线索上，有效解决了奖励函数在分布偏移下的泛化问题，为机器人学习提供了更鲁棒的奖励函数框架

Abstract: Robots learn reward functions from user demonstrations, but these rewards often fail to generalize to new environments. This failure occurs because learned rewards latch onto spurious correlations in training data rather than the underlying human intent that demonstrations represent. Existing methods leverage visual or semantic similarity to improve robustness, yet these surface-level cues often diverge from what humans actually care about. We present Generalizing Intent for Flexible Test-Time Rewards (GIFT), a framework that grounds reward generalization in human intent rather than surface cues. GIFT leverages language models to infer high-level intent from user demonstrations by contrasting preferred with non-preferred behaviors. At deployment, GIFT maps novel test states to behaviorally equivalent training states via intent-conditioned similarity, enabling learned rewards to generalize across distribution shifts without retraining. We evaluate GIFT on tabletop manipulation tasks with new objects and layouts. Across four simulated tasks with over 50 unseen objects, GIFT consistently outperforms visual and semantic similarity baselines in test-time pairwise win rate and state-alignment F1 score. Real-world experiments on a 7-DoF Franka Panda robot demonstrate that GIFT reliably transfers to physical settings. Further discussion can be found at https://mit-clear-lab.github.io/GIFT/

</details>


### [150] [Task-Agnostic Exoskeleton Control Supports Elderly Joint Energetics during Hip-Intensive Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22580)
*Jiefu Zhang,Nikhil V. Divekar,Chandramouli Krishnan,Robert D. Gregg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了一种任务无关的髋关节外骨骼控制器，在老年人群中验证其有效性，可减少髋关节生物功24.7%，增强总功率输出，支持老年人活动能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年人活动能力下降常伴随关节动力学重新分配，即通过增加髋关节负荷来补偿踝关节功能减退。但矛盾的是，这种代偿性转移通常与年龄相关的最大髋关节功率下降同时发生。虽然机器人外骨骼能提供即时能量效益，但传统控制策略仅限于特定任务（如稳态行走），无法完全反映家庭和社区的实际活动需求。

Method: 实现了一种任务无关的髋关节外骨骼控制器，该控制器对关节功率具有内在敏感性。在8名老年人中验证其有效性，测试包括平地行走、斜坡上行、爬楼梯和坐站转换等髋关节密集型活动。控制器能够高精度匹配生物功率曲线（平均余弦相似度0.89）。

Result: 外骨骼辅助显著减少了矢状面生物正功：髋关节减少24.7%，下肢整体减少9.3%。同时增强了峰值总（生物+外骨骼）髋关节功率，并降低了峰值生物髋关节功率。在多种活动中均表现出良好性能。

Conclusion: 髋关节外骨骼通过减少生物功可能增强耐力，通过增加总功率可能提高功能储备，是一种有前景的生物力学干预手段，可支持老年人的活动能力。

Abstract: Age-related mobility decline is frequently accompanied by a redistribution of joint kinetics, where older adults compensate for reduced ankle function by increasing demand on the hip. Paradoxically, this compensatory shift typically coincides with age-related reductions in maximal hip power. Although robotic exoskeletons can provide immediate energetic benefits, conventional control strategies have limited previous studies in this population to specific tasks such as steady-state walking, which do not fully reflect mobility demands in the home and community. Here, we implement a task-agnostic hip exoskeleton controller that is inherently sensitive to joint power and validate its efficacy in eight older adults. Across a battery of hip-intensive activities that included level walking, ramp ascent, stair climbing, and sit-to-stand transitions, the exoskeleton matched biological power profiles with high accuracy (mean cosine similarity 0.89). Assistance significantly reduced sagittal plane biological positive work by 24.7% at the hip and by 9.3% for the lower limb, while simultaneously augmenting peak total (biological + exoskeleton) hip power and reducing peak biological hip power. These results suggest that hip exoskeletons can potentially enhance endurance through biological work reduction, and increase functional reserve through total power augmentation, serving as a promising biomechanical intervention to support older adults' mobility.

</details>


### [151] [Trajectory Generation for Underactuated Soft Robot Manipulators using Discrete Elastic Rod Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22604)
*Beibei Liu,Akua K. Dickson,Ran Jing,Andrew P. Sabelhaus*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向控制的离散弹性杆动力学重构方法，用于软体机器人轨迹生成，在保持物理精度的同时提高计算效率，并通过气动软体机器人实验验证了优于恒定曲率基线的轨迹跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人因其柔顺性适合接触丰富的任务，但柔顺性也使得精确且可处理的建模变得困难。现有方法在物理精度和计算效率之间存在不平衡：连续杆模型提供物理精度但计算量大，而降阶近似提高效率却牺牲了建模保真度。

Method: 提出了离散弹性杆动力学的控制导向重构方法，生成具有这些动力学的轨迹。该重构产生控制仿射表示，同时保留某些基于第一原理的力-变形关系，使生成的轨迹既动态可行又与底层驱动假设一致。

Result: 在气动软体机器人肢体上进行了实验验证，硬件结果表明，在复杂驱动条件下，该方法的轨迹跟踪性能持续优于基于恒定曲率的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过控制导向的离散弹性杆动力学重构，实现了在保持物理精度的同时提高计算效率的软体机器人轨迹生成方法，为软体机器人的动态可行轨迹规划提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Soft robots are well suited for contact-rich tasks due to their compliance, yet this property makes accurate and tractable modeling challenging. Planning motions with dynamically-feasible trajectories requires models that capture arbitrary deformations, remain computationally efficient, and are compatible with underactuation. However, existing approaches balance these properties unevenly: continuum rod models provide physical accuracy but are computationally demanding, while reduced-order approximations improve efficiency at the cost of modeling fidelity. To address this, our work introduces a control-oriented reformulation of Discrete Elastic Rod (DER) dynamics for soft robots, and a method to generate trajectories with these dynamics. The proposed formulation yields a control-affine representation while preserving certain first-principles force-deformation relationships. As a result, the generated trajectories are both dynamically feasible and consistent with the underlying actuation assumptions. We present our trajectory generation framework and validate it experimentally on a pneumatic soft robotic limb. Hardware results demonstrate consistently improved trajectory tracking performance over a constant-curvature-based baseline, particularly under complex actuation conditions.

</details>


### [152] [Variable-Resolution Virtual Maps for Autonomous Exploration with Unmanned Surface Vehicles (USVs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22667)
*Ye Li,Yewei Huang,Wenlong GaoZhang,Alberto Quattrini Li,Brendan Englot,Yuanchang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可变分辨率虚拟地图（VRVM）方法，使用自适应四叉树和双变量高斯虚拟地标高效表示地图不确定性，结合EM规划器平衡探索与利用，在GNSS降级的近岸环境中实现更安全的行为和更好的计算资源利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近岸水域无人水面艇自主探索面临GNSS降级、环境引起的定位不确定性以及有限机载计算资源的挑战。现有虚拟地图方法使用固定分辨率工作空间离散化，存储和计算成本随规模增长而急剧增加，在大规模近岸环境中效率低下，且过度重视特征稀疏的开放水域区域可能导致SLAM失败风险增加。

Method: 提出可变分辨率虚拟地图（VRVM），采用自适应四叉树结构，在单元格中放置双变量高斯虚拟地标表示地图不确定性。自适应四叉树实现面积加权的不确定性表示：对远场区域保持粗粒度虚拟地标故意不确定，对信息密集区域分配更高分辨率，降低地图估值对树局部细化的敏感性。采用期望最大化（EM）规划器评估前沿的位姿和地图不确定性，平衡探索与利用。

Result: 在VRX Gazebo模拟器中，使用真实码头环境在不同测试场景（探索难度递增）下评估VRVM与多种最先进探索算法。结果表明，在GNSS降级的近岸环境中，该方法提供更安全的行为和更好的机载计算资源利用。

Conclusion: VRVM方法通过自适应分辨率表示地图不确定性，有效解决了现有虚拟地图方法在存储计算效率和探索-利用平衡方面的局限性，为GNSS降级近岸环境中的无人水面艇自主探索提供了更高效可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous exploration by unmanned surface vehicles (USVs) in near-shore waters requires reliable localisation and consistent mapping over extended areas, but this is challenged by GNSS degradation, environment-induced localisation uncertainty, and limited on-board computation. Virtual map-based methods explicitly model localisation and mapping uncertainty by tightly coupling factor-graph SLAM with a map uncertainty criterion. However, their storage and computational costs scale poorly with fixed-resolution workspace discretisations, leading to inefficiency in large near-shore environments. Moreover, overvaluing feature-sparse open-water regions can increase the risk of SLAM failure as a result of imbalance between exploration and exploitation. To address these limitations, we propose a Variable-Resolution Virtual Map (VRVM), a computationally efficient method for representing map uncertainty using bivariate Gaussian virtual landmarks placed in the cells of an adaptive quadtree. The adaptive quadtree enables an area-weighted uncertainty representation that keeps coarse, far-field virtual landmarks deliberately uncertain while allocating higher resolution to information-dense regions, and reduces the sensitivity of the map valuation to local refinements of the tree. An expectation-maximisation (EM) planner is adopted to evaluate pose and map uncertainty along frontiers using the VRVM, balancing exploration and exploitation. We evaluate VRVM against several state-of-the-art exploration algorithms in the VRX Gazebo simulator, using a realistic marina environment across different testing scenarios with an increasing level of exploration difficulty. The results indicate that our method offers safer behaviour and better utilisation of on-board computation in GNSS-degraded near-shore environments.

</details>


### [153] [SG-VLA: Learning Spatially-Grounded Vision-Language-Action Models for Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22760)
*Ruisen Tu,Arth Shukla,Sohyun Yoo,Xuanlin Li,Junxi Li,Jianwen Xie,Hao Su,Zhuowen Tu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出通过辅助任务协同训练和多模态输入增强来学习空间基础视觉-语言-动作模型，以改进家庭环境中的移动操作性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言-动作模型在复杂家庭环境中的表现不理想，移动操作需要全局场景布局、细粒度几何和高维连续动作的推理，标准模仿学习不足

Method: 采用多模态输入增强（多视角RGB、深度线索、短时历史）和辅助解码器协同训练，重建可解释的中间信号（全局机器人位置、关节配置、抓取可操作性、目标物体相对位姿、分割掩码）

Result: 在家庭重排任务评估中，该方法在拾取、放置、打开和关闭操作上取得一致改进，显著优于直接模仿学习

Conclusion: 通过辅助任务和多模态学习的空间基础为扩展VLA模型实现通用家庭机器人提供了有力方向

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models show promise for robotic control, yet performance in complex household environments remains sub-optimal. Mobile manipulation requires reasoning about global scene layout, fine-grained geometry, and high-dimensional continuous actions, making standard imitation learning insufficient. We introduce a framework for learning spatially-grounded VLA models that strengthens perception and representation through auxiliary task co-training and multi-modal input enhancement. Our method addresses the challenge of controlling a 13-dimensional action space involving coordinated base motion, arm articulation, and gripper actuation. To enrich spatial understanding, the model incorporates multi-view RGB observations, depth cues, and short temporal history, providing perspectives of both global scene structure and local manipulation context. To improve representation quality, we co-train auxiliary decoders that reconstruct interpretable intermediate signals - including global robot position, joint configurations, grasp affordances, target-object relative pose, and segmentation masks - from shared visual-language features. These objectives provide dense supervision that encourages the backbone to develop spatially grounded, manipulation-aware latent representations. Through extensive evaluation on home rearrangement tasks, our approach achieves consistent improvements across picking, placing, opening, and closing operations, substantially outperforming direct imitation learning. Our findings suggest that spatial grounding through auxiliary and multi-modal learning provides a strong direction for scaling VLA models toward general-purpose domestic robots.

</details>


### [154] [CATNAV: Cached Vision-Language Traversability for Efficient Zero-Shot Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22800)
*Aditya Potnis,Francisco Affonso,Shreya Gummadi,Naveen Kumar Uppalapati,Girish Chowdhary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CATNAV：基于多模态大语言模型的零样本、具身感知导航框架，通过视觉语义缓存减少85.7%的VLM查询，在非结构化环境中实现更高的目标到达率和更少的行为约束违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非结构化环境中导航需要根据机器人的物理能力评估穿越风险，这一挑战因机器人具体形态而异。现有方法缺乏对机器人具体形态的适应性，且在线视觉语言模型查询成本高昂。

Method: 1. 基于多模态LLM的零样本、具身感知成本图生成，无需任务特定训练；2. 视觉语义缓存机制，检测场景新颖性并重用语义相似帧的风险评估；3. VLM轨迹选择模块，通过视觉推理评估候选轨迹，选择最安全路径。

Result: 在四足机器人室内外非结构化环境测试中，相比最先进的视觉-语言-动作基线方法，CATNAV在五个导航任务中平均目标到达率提高10个百分点，行为约束违规减少33%，在线VLM查询减少85.7%。

Conclusion: CATNAV通过零样本、具身感知的成本图生成和高效的视觉语义缓存，实现了在非结构化环境中的高效、安全导航，显著减少了计算开销并提高了导航性能。

Abstract: Navigating unstructured environments requires assessing traversal risk relative to a robot's physical capabilities, a challenge that varies across embodiments. We present CATNAV, a cost-aware traversability navigation framework that leverages multimodal LLMs for zero-shot, embodiment-aware costmap generation without task-specific training. We introduce a visuosemantic caching mechanism that detects scene novelty and reuses prior risk assessments for semantically similar frames, reducing online VLM queries by 85.7%. Furthermore, we introduce a VLM-based trajectory selection module that evaluates proposals through visual reasoning to choose the safest path given behavioral constraints. We evaluate CATNAV on a quadruped robot across indoor and outdoor unstructured environments, comparing against state-of-the-art vision-language-action baselines. Across five navigation tasks, CATNAV achieves 10 percentage point higher average goal-reaching rate and 33% fewer behavioral constraint violations.

</details>


### [155] [DecompGrind: A Decomposition Framework for Robotic Grinding via Cutting-Surface Planning and Contact-Force Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22859)
*Shunsuke Araki,Takumi Hachimine,Yuki Saito,Kouhei Ohnishi,Jun Morimoto,Takamitsu Masubara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DecompGrind框架将机器人磨削分解为全局切削表面规划和局部接触力适应，通过几何分析处理形状过渡，通过少量演示学习局部力控制，实现不同形状和材料硬度工件的高效磨削。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人磨削自动化面临挑战：不同形状和材料硬度的工件导致去除阻力变化，难以准确估计去除阻力和建模形状过渡，基于学习的方法需要大量训练数据覆盖多样加工条件。

Method: 提出DecompGrind框架，包含两个组件：1) 全局切削表面规划(GCSP)：通过几何分析当前和目标形状确定去除形状，无需学习；2) 局部接触力适应(LCFA)：使用双边控制模仿学习在磨削每个去除形状时学习接触力适应策略。

Result: 实验使用机器人磨削系统和3D打印工件，证明DecompGrind能够高效磨削不同形状和材料硬度的工件，同时保持安全的接触力水平。

Conclusion: 通过将机器人磨削分解为全局形状规划和局部力适应，DecompGrind框架实现了高效自动化磨削，仅需少量演示学习局部力控制，而通过几何分析处理复杂的全局形状过渡。

Abstract: Robotic grinding is widely used for shaping workpieces in manufacturing, but it remains difficult to automate this process efficiently. In particular, efficiently grinding workpieces of different shapes and material hardness is challenging because removal resistance varies with local contact conditions. Moreover, it is difficult to achieve accurate estimation of removal resistance and analytical modeling of shape transition, and learning-based approaches often require large amounts of training data to cover diverse processing conditions. To address these challenges, we decompose robotic grinding into two components: removal-shape planning and contact-force adaptation. Based on this formulation, we propose DecompGrind, a framework that combines Global Cutting-Surface Planning (GCSP) and Local Contact-Force Adaptation (LCFA). GCSP determines removal shapes through geometric analysis of the current and target shapes without learning, while LCFA learns a contact-force adaptation policy using bilateral control-based imitation learning during the grinding of each removal shape. This decomposition restricts learning to local contact-force adaptation, allowing the policy to be learned from a small number of demonstrations, while handling global shape transition geometrically. Experiments using a robotic grinding system and 3D-printed workpieces demonstrate efficient robotic grinding of workpieces having different shapes and material hardness while maintaining safe levels of contact force.

</details>


### [156] [Grounding Sim-to-Real Generalization in Dexterous Manipulation: An Empirical Study with Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22876)
*Ruixing Jin,Zicheng Zhu,Ruixiang Ouyang,Sheng Xu,Bo Yue,Zhizheng Wu,Guiliang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过超过1万次真实世界试验，系统评估了仿真到真实世界迁移在灵巧操作任务中的四个关键维度：多级域随机化、照片级渲染、物理真实建模和强化学习更新，并发布了机器人平台和评估协议作为标准化基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作通用策略学习通常依赖大规模数据集，但真实世界数据收集成本高昂。虽然仿真生成合成数据是实用替代方案，但存在显著的仿真到真实世界分布差异。现有研究缺乏在真实世界操作任务中系统评估这些方法性能的实证研究，特别是对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）等通用策略的影响。

Method: 设计了全面的评估协议来量化操作任务的真实世界性能，考虑背景、光照、干扰物、物体类型和空间特征等关键变化。通过超过1万次真实世界试验，实证检验了仿真到真实世界泛化的四个主要决定因素：多级域随机化、照片级渲染、物理真实建模和强化学习更新。

Result: 通过大规模实验获得了仿真到真实世界迁移的关键见解。研究发布了机器人平台和评估协议供公开访问，以促进独立验证，为灵巧操作策略建立了现实且标准化的基准。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了仿真到真实世界迁移在真实世界操作任务中缺乏系统评估的研究空白，特别是对通用策略的影响。通过建立标准化评估协议和发布实验平台，为未来研究提供了可验证的基准，推动了灵巧操作策略的仿真到真实世界迁移研究。

Abstract: Learning a generalist control policy for dexterous manipulation typically relies on large-scale datasets. Given the high cost of real-world data collection, a practical alternative is to generate synthetic data through simulation. However, the resulting synthetic data often exhibits a significant gap from real-world distributions. While many prior studies have proposed algorithms to bridge the Sim-to-Real discrepancy, there remains a lack of principled research that grounds these methods in real-world manipulation tasks, particularly their performance on generalist policies such as Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. In this study, we empirically examine the primary determinants of Sim-to-Real generalization across four dimensions: multi-level domain randomization, photorealistic rendering, physics-realistic modeling, and reinforcement learning updates. To support this study, we design a comprehensive evaluation protocol to quantify the real-world performance of manipulation tasks. The protocol accounts for key variations in background, lighting, distractors, object types, and spatial features. Through experiments involving over 10k real-world trials, we derive critical insights into Sim-to-Real transfer. To inform and advance future studies, we release both the robotic platforms and the evaluation protocol for public access to facilitate independent verification, thereby establishing a realistic and standardized benchmark for dexterous manipulation policies.

</details>


### [157] [Agile-VLA: Few-Shot Industrial Pose Rectification via Implicit Affordance Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22899)
*Teng Yan,Zhengyang Pei,Chengyu Shi,Yue Yu,Yikun Chen,Zilong Zhu,Zelin Fang,Kaile Guo,Zihang Wang,Peigen Tian,Bingzhuo Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Agile-VLA是一个用于边缘设备的分层框架，通过隐式可供性锚定机制将几何视觉线索直接映射到结构化动作基元，减少对高延迟语义推理的依赖，实现感知与控制解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的边缘平台上部署视觉-语言-动作模型时，高延迟的语义推理与动态操作所需的高频控制之间存在根本冲突，需要解决边缘机器人学习中的频率不匹配问题。

Method: 提出Agile-VLA分层框架，采用隐式可供性锚定机制，将质心和边缘关键点等几何视觉线索直接映射到结构化参数化动作基元；通过异步双流架构实现感知（10Hz）与控制（50Hz）解耦。

Result: 在标准6自由度机械臂上的实验表明，Agile-VLA仅通过5次演示就能稳健地校正复杂不规则工件，利用外部灵巧性在边缘设备上实现高效操作。

Conclusion: Agile-VLA通过几何驱动的动作基元和感知-控制解耦架构，有效解决了边缘设备上VLA模型的高延迟语义推理与高频控制需求之间的冲突，为工业姿态重定向任务提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Deploying Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models on resource-constrained edge platforms encounters a fundamental conflict between high-latency semantic inference and the high-frequency control required for dynamic manipulation. To address the challenge, this paper presents Agile-VLA, a hierarchical framework designed for industrial pose reorientation tasks on edge devices such as the NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano. The core innovation is an Implicit Affordance Anchoring mechanism that directly maps geometric visual cues, specifically centroid and rim keypoint anchors, into structured parametric action primitives, thereby substantially reducing reliance on high-latency semantic inference during closed-loop control. By decoupling perception (10 Hz) from control (50 Hz) via an asynchronous dual-stream architecture, the system effectively mitigates the frequency mismatch inherent in edge-based robot learning. Experimental results on a standard 6-DoF manipulator demonstrate that Agile-VLA achieves robust rectification of complex, irregular workpieces using only 5-shot demonstrations through extrinsic dexterity.

</details>


### [158] [Tightly-Coupled Radar-Visual-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23052)
*Morten Nissov,Mohit Singh,Kostas Alexis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种紧密耦合的视觉-惯性-雷达里程计方法，通过融合图像特征、雷达多普勒测量和IMU数据，在恶劣环境（黑暗、低纹理、有遮挡）中实现鲁棒的状态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉惯性里程计在黑暗、低纹理、有遮挡环境中性能受限，而雷达惯性里程计虽然对这些视觉挑战具有鲁棒性，但信息密度较低且长期精度较差。需要一种能结合两者优势的方法。

Method: 采用紧密耦合方式融合图像特征、雷达多普勒测量和IMU测量，使用迭代扩展卡尔曼滤波器实时处理，利用雷达测距数据增强视觉特征深度初始化。

Result: 在室内外飞行实验中进行了评估，测试了黑暗、雾、快速飞行等挑战性条件，充分证明了该方法在各种环境条件和轨迹动态下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出的视觉-惯性-雷达里程计方法通过紧密耦合多传感器数据，实现了在恶劣环境条件下的鲁棒状态估计，克服了单一传感器系统的局限性。

Abstract: Visual-Inertial Odometry (VIO) is a staple for reliable state estimation on constrained and lightweight platforms due to its versatility and demonstrated performance. However, pertinent challenges regarding robust operation in dark, low-texture, obscured environments complicate the use of such methods. Alternatively, Frequency Modulated Continuous Wave (FMCW) radars, and by extension Radar-Inertial Odometry (RIO), offer robustness to these visual challenges, albeit at the cost of reduced information density and worse long-term accuracy. To address these limitations, this work combines the two in a tightly coupled manner, enabling the resulting method to operate robustly regardless of environmental conditions or trajectory dynamics. The proposed method fuses image features, radar Doppler measurements, and Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) measurements within an Iterated Extended Kalman Filter (IEKF) in real-time, with radar range data augmenting the visual feature depth initialization. The method is evaluated through flight experiments conducted in both indoor and outdoor environments, as well as through challenges to both exteroceptive modalities (such as darkness, fog, or fast flight), thoroughly demonstrating its robustness. The implementation of the proposed method is available at: https://github.com/ntnu-arl/radvio .

</details>


### [159] [AirSimAG: A High-Fidelity Simulation Platform for Air-Ground Collaborative Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23079)
*Yangjie Cui,Xin Dong,Boyang Gao,Jinwu Xiang,Daochun Li,Zhan Tu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AirsimAG是一个基于AirSim框架构建的高保真空地协同仿真平台，专门用于无人机和无人地面车辆的异构多智能体协同仿真，支持同步多智能体仿真和异构传感控制接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的仿真平台主要针对单智能体动力学设计，缺乏专门用于交互式空地协同仿真的框架。随着空间智能的发展，无人机和无人地面车辆的异构多智能体系统在复杂应用中展现出强大潜力，需要专门的仿真平台来支持这类研究。

Method: 基于AirSim框架进行深度定制，构建了AirsimAG平台。该平台支持同步多智能体仿真，为无人机-无人地面车辆系统提供异构传感和控制接口。通过设计一系列代表性的空地协同任务来展示平台能力。

Result: 开发了AirsimAG仿真平台，支持无人机和无人地面车辆的协同仿真。设计了包括建图、规划、跟踪、编队和探索在内的代表性空地协同任务，并通过定量分析验证了平台在多智能体协调和跨模态数据一致性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: AirsimAG是一个有效的高保真空地协同仿真平台，能够支持无人机和无人地面车辆的异构多智能体协同研究。平台已公开可用，为相关领域的研究提供了重要工具。

Abstract: As spatial intelligence continues to evolve, heterogeneous multi-agent systems-particularly the collaboration between Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) and Unmanned Ground Vehicles (UGVs), have demonstrated strong potential in complex applications such as search and rescue, urban surveillance, and environmental monitoring. However, existing simulation platforms are primarily designed for single-agent dynamics and lack dedicated frameworks for interactive air-ground collaborative simulation. In this paper, we present AirsimAG, a high-fidelity air-ground collaborative simulation platform built upon an extensively customized AirSim framework. The platform enables synchronized multi-agent simulation and supports heterogeneous sensing and control interfaces for UAV-UGV systems. To demonstrate its capabilities, we design a set of representative air-ground collaborative tasks, including mapping, planning, tracking, formation, and exploration. We further provide quantitative analyses based on these tasks to illustrate the platform effectiveness in supporting multi-agent coordination and cross-modal data consistency. The AirsimAG simulation platform is publicly available at https://github.com/BIULab-BUAA/AirSimAG.

</details>


### [160] [Active Robotic Perception for Disease Detection and Mapping in Apple Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23112)
*Hayden Feddock,Francisco Yandun,Srđan Aćimović,Abhisesh Silwal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出用于休眠苹果树病害检测的自主移动主动感知系统，针对火疫病，结合立体RGB、深度估计、实例分割和语义3D建图，评估三种视点规划策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模果园生产需要及时精确的病害监测，但人工巡查劳动密集且成本高昂，导致病害发现晚且空间分辨率粗。需要自动化系统进行精准病害定位和建图。

Method: 系统集成闪光照明立体RGB感知、实时深度估计、实例级分割和置信感知语义3D建图。在统一感知-行动循环中评估三种视点规划策略：确定性几何基线、最大化未知空间减少的体素化最佳视点规划器、优先低置信度症状区域的语义最佳视点规划器。

Result: 在模拟实验中，语义规划器在30个视点后获得最高F1分数(0.6106)，体素规划器获得最高ROI覆盖率(85.82%)。在实验室设置中，语义规划器获得最高最终F1分数(0.9058)，两种最佳视点规划器相比基线显著提升覆盖率。

Conclusion: 该系统能够可靠定位和建图病害症状，为田间评估奠定基础。语义规划器在症状检测方面表现最佳，体素规划器在覆盖范围方面最优，展示了主动感知系统在果园病害监测中的潜力。

Abstract: Large-scale orchard production requires timely and precise disease monitoring, yet routine manual scouting is labor-intensive and financially impractical at the scale of modern operations. As a result, disease outbreaks are often detected late and tracked at coarse spatial resolutions, typically at the orchard-block level. We present an autonomous mobile active perception system for targeted disease detection and mapping in dormant apple trees, demonstrated on one of the most devastating diseases affecting apple today -- fire blight. The system integrates flash-illuminated stereo RGB sensing, real-time depth estimation, instance-level segmentation, and confidence-aware semantic 3D mapping to achieve precise localization of disease symptoms. Semantic predictions are fused into the volumetric occupancy map representation enabling the tracking of both occupancy and per-voxel semantic confidence, building actionable spatial maps for growers. To actively refine observations within complex canopies, we evaluate three viewpoint planning strategies within a unified perception-action loop: a deterministic geometric baseline, a volumetric next-best-view planner that maximizes unknown-space reduction, and a semantic next-best-view planner that prioritizes low-confidence symptomatic regions. Experiments on a fabricated lab tree and five simulated symptomatic trees demonstrate reliable symptom localization and mapping as a precursor to a field evaluation. In simulation, the semantic planner achieves the highest F1 score (0.6106) after 30 viewpoints, while the volumetric planner achieves the highest ROI coverage (85.82\%). In the lab setting, the semantic planner attains the highest final F1 (0.9058), with both next-best-view planners substantially improving coverage over the baseline.

</details>


### [161] [PHANTOM Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23152)
*Teng Yan,Jiongxu Chen,Qixiang Hua,Yue Yu,Zihang Wang,Yaohua Liu,Bingzhuo Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PHANTOM Hand：一种混合精确增强柔顺的欠驱动灵巧手，通过统一框架实现精确运动规划与稳定抓取的结合，解决了传统肌腱驱动欠驱动手运动学不可预测和力传递非线性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统肌腱驱动欠驱动手在自适应抓取方面表现出色，但存在运动学不可预测性和高度非线性力传递问题。这种模糊性限制了其执行精确自由运动成形和复杂操作任务中可靠载荷传递的能力。

Method: 提出PHANTOM Hand系统（1:1人形尺寸，6个执行器，15个自由度），建立统一框架连接精确解析成形与鲁棒柔顺抓取。通过推导物理几何的稀疏映射并集成基于力学的补偿模型，有效抑制弹簧反张力和肌腱弹性引起的运动学漂移。

Result: 实验验证：1) 运动学分析显示整个工作空间内亚度级全局精度；2) 静态表达能力测试展示复杂手势；3) 多样化抓取实验覆盖强力、精密和工具使用类别；4) 指尖力定量表征。系统成功结合解析运动学精度与连续可预测力输出。

Conclusion: PHANTOM手成功将解析运动学精度与连续可预测力输出相结合，显著扩展了欠驱动手的载荷能力和灵巧性。所有硬件设计和控制脚本完全开源，推动欠驱动操作生态系统发展。

Abstract: Tendon-driven underactuated hands excel in adaptive grasping but often suffer from kinematic unpredictability and highly non-linear force transmission. This ambiguity limits their ability to perform precise free-motion shaping and deliver reliable payloads for complex manipulation tasks. To address this, we introduce the PHANTOM Hand (Hybrid Precision-Augmented Compliance): a modular, 1:1 human-scale system featuring 6 actuators and 15 degrees of freedom (DoFs).
  We propose a unified framework that bridges the gap between precise analytic shaping and robust compliant grasping. By deriving a sparse mapping from physical geometry and integrating a mechanics-based compensation model, we effectively suppress kinematic drift caused by spring counter-tension and tendon elasticity. This approach achieves sub-degree kinematic reproducibility for free-motion planning while retaining the inherent mechanical compliance required for stable physical interaction.
  Experimental validation confirms the system's capabilities through (1) kinematic analysis verifying sub-degree global accuracy across the workspace; (2) static expressibility tests demonstrating complex hand gestures; (3) diverse grasping experiments covering power, precision, and tool-use categories; and (4) quantitative fingertip force characterization. The results demonstrate that the PHANTOM hand successfully combines analytic kinematic precision with continuous, predictable force output, significantly expanding the payload and dexterity of underactuated hands. To drive the development of the underactuated manipulation ecosystem, all hardware designs and control scripts are fully open-sourced for community engagement.

</details>


### [162] [LiZIP: An Auto-Regressive Compression Framework for LiDAR Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23162)
*Aditya Shibu,Kayvan Karim,Claudio Zito*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LiZIP：基于神经预测编码的轻量级近无损零漂移LiDAR点云压缩框架，在保持高精度的同时显著提升压缩比


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆LiDAR传感器产生海量数据，对实时处理和V2X传输造成瓶颈。现有无损压缩方法存在局限性：行业标准算法（如LASzip）缺乏适应性，而深度学习方法计算成本过高。

Method: 提出LiZIP框架，基于神经预测编码，使用紧凑的多层感知机（MLP）从局部上下文预测点坐标，仅编码稀疏残差，实现轻量级近无损零漂移压缩。

Result: 在NuScenes和Argoverse数据集上评估，相比行业标准LASzip减少7.5%-14.8%文件大小，比Google Draco（24位量化配置）提升8.8%-11.3%，比通用GZip算法减少38%-48%。在未见过的Argoverse数据集上无需重新训练即展现泛化能力。

Conclusion: LiZIP为带宽受限的V2X应用和大规模云存储提供了高效压缩解决方案，在保持高几何精度的同时显著提升压缩性能。

Abstract: The massive volume of data generated by LiDAR sensors in autonomous vehicles creates a bottleneck for real-time processing and vehicle-to-everything (V2X) transmission. Existing lossless compression methods often force a trade-off: industry standard algorithms (e.g., LASzip) lack adaptability, while deep learning approaches suffer from prohibitive computational costs. This paper proposes LiZIP, a lightweight, near-lossless zero-drift compression framework based on neural predictive coding. By utilizing a compact Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) to predict point coordinates from local context, LiZIP efficiently encodes only the sparse residuals.
  We evaluate LiZIP on the NuScenes and Argoverse datasets, benchmarking against GZip, LASzip, and Google Draco (configured with 24-bit quantization to serve as a high-precision geometric baseline). Results demonstrate that LiZIP consistently achieves superior compression ratios across varying environments. The proposed system achieves a 7.5%-14.8% reduction in file size compared to the industry-standard LASzip and outperforms Google Draco by 8.8%-11.3% across diverse datasets. Furthermore, the system demonstrates generalization capabilities on the unseen Argoverse dataset without retraining. Against the general purpose GZip algorithm, LiZIP achieves a reduction of 38%-48%. This efficiency offers a distinct advantage for bandwidth constrained V2X applications and large scale cloud archival.

</details>


### [163] [Path Planning and Reinforcement Learning-Driven Control of On-Orbit Free-Flying Multi-Arm Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23182)
*Álvaro Belmonte-Baeza,José Luis Ramón,Leonard Felicetti,Miguel Cazorla,Jorge Pomares*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种结合轨迹优化(TO)和强化学习(RL)的混合方法，用于自由飞行多臂机器人在轨服务的运动规划与控制，通过TO生成可行高效路径，RL实现不确定条件下的自适应轨迹跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在轨服务场景中自由飞行多臂机器人的运动规划与控制挑战，包括动态和运动学约束、环境不确定性、运动耦合等问题，提升空间机器人系统在动态不确定环境中的自主性和性能。

Method: 采用混合方法：轨迹优化(TO)负责生成考虑动态和运动学约束的可行高效路径，优化机械臂运动和推进器力；强化学习(RL)负责自适应轨迹跟踪，处理不确定性和动态交互；多臂机器人设计配备推进器实现精确本体控制。

Result: 通过综合仿真验证了混合方法的有效性和鲁棒性。在两个案例研究中（初始接触的表面运动和需要表面逼近的自由浮动场景），混合方法均优于传统策略。推进器显著提升了运动平滑性、安全性和操作效率，RL策略能有效跟踪TO生成的轨迹。

Conclusion: TO和RL的结合将精确的任务特定规划与鲁棒适应性优势相结合，为提升空间机器人系统在动态不确定环境中的自主性和有效性奠定了坚实基础，能够应对运动耦合、环境扰动和动态控制需求等挑战。

Abstract: This paper presents a hybrid approach that integrates trajectory optimization (TO) and reinforcement learning (RL) for motion planning and control of free-flying multi-arm robots in on-orbit servicing scenarios. The proposed system integrates TO for generating feasible, efficient paths while accounting for dynamic and kinematic constraints, and RL for adaptive trajectory tracking under uncertainties. The multi-arm robot design, equipped with thrusters for precise body control, enables redundancy and stability in complex space operations. TO optimizes arm motions and thruster forces, reducing reliance on the arms for stabilization and enhancing maneuverability. RL further refines this by leveraging model-free control to adapt to dynamic interactions and disturbances. The experimental results validated through comprehensive simulations demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed hybrid approach. Two case studies are explored: surface motion with initial contact and a free-floating scenario requiring surface approximation. In both cases, the hybrid method outperforms traditional strategies. In particular, the thrusters notably enhance motion smoothness, safety, and operational efficiency. The RL policy effectively tracks TO-generated trajectories, handling high-dimensional action spaces and dynamic mismatches. This integration of TO and RL combines the strengths of precise, task-specific planning with robust adaptability, ensuring high performance in the uncertain and dynamic conditions characteristic of space environments. By addressing challenges such as motion coupling, environmental disturbances, and dynamic control requirements, this framework establishes a strong foundation for advancing the autonomy and effectiveness of space robotic systems.

</details>


### [164] [AeroScene: Progressive Scene Synthesis for Aerial Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23224)
*Nghia Vu,Tuong Do,Dzung Tran,Binh X. Nguyen,Hoan Nguyen,Erman Tjiputra,Quang D. Tran,Hai-Nguyen Nguyen,Anh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AeroScene：用于无人机仿真的分层扩散模型，通过渐进式3D场景合成生成物理合理、语义一致的场景，显著优于现有方法，并创建了可直接集成到NVIDIA Isaac Sim的大规模数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型在多个领域已显示出巨大影响，但在机器人场景合成方面仍未被充分探索。无人机仿真环境尤其依赖人工创建，耗时且难以扩展，需要自动化、可扩展的场景生成方法。

Method: 提出AeroScene分层扩散模型，采用层次感知标记化和多分支特征提取，在全局布局和局部细节上进行推理，确保物理合理性和语义一致性，适用于无人机导航、着陆和栖息等任务。

Result: 在新收集的数据集和公共基准测试中，AeroScene显著优于现有方法。生成了包含1000多个物理就绪、高保真3D场景的大规模数据集，可直接集成到NVIDIA Isaac Sim中，并在下游无人机导航任务中展示了其实用性。

Conclusion: AeroScene为无人机仿真提供了一种高效、可扩展的场景生成解决方案，通过分层扩散模型实现了物理合理、语义一致的3D场景合成，为机器人任务创建了可直接使用的仿真环境。

Abstract: Generative models have shown substantial impact across multiple domains, their potential for scene synthesis remains underexplored in robotics. This gap is more evident in drone simulators, where simulation environments still rely heavily on manual efforts, which are time-consuming to create and difficult to scale. In this work, we introduce AeroScene, a hierarchical diffusion model for progressive 3D scene synthesis. Our approach leverages hierarchy-aware tokenization and multi-branch feature extraction to reason across both global layouts and local details, ensuring physical plausibility and semantic consistency. This makes AeroScene particularly suited for generating realistic scenes for aerial robotics tasks such as navigation, landing, and perching. We demonstrate its effectiveness through extensive experiments on our newly collected dataset and a public benchmark, showing that AeroScene significantly outperforms prior methods. Furthermore, we use AeroScene to generate a large-scale dataset of over 1,000 physics-ready, high fidelity 3D scenes that can be directly integrated into NVIDIA Isaac Sim. Finally, we illustrate the utility of these generated environments on downstream drone navigation tasks. Our code and dataset are publicly available at aioz-ai.github.io/AeroScene/

</details>


### [165] [Efficient Hybrid SE(3)-Equivariant Visuomotor Flow Policy via Spherical Harmonics for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23227)
*Qinglun Zhang,Shen Cheng,Tian Dan,Haoqiang Fan,Guanghui Liu,Shuaicheng Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: E3Flow是一个新颖的框架，首次将高效的整流流与稳定的多模态等变学习相结合，解决了现有等变方法计算强度高、依赖单模态输入以及与快速采样方法结合不稳定的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有等变方法虽然提高了数据效率，但存在三个关键限制：1）计算强度高；2）依赖单模态输入；3）与快速采样方法结合时不稳定。这些限制阻碍了等变方法在实际机器人策略学习中的应用。

Method: E3Flow基于球谐表示确保严格的SO(3)等变性，引入新颖的不变特征增强模块（FEM）动态融合混合视觉模态（点云和图像），将丰富的视觉线索注入球谐特征中。该框架首次将高效的整流流与稳定的多模态等变学习相统一。

Result: 在MimicGen的8个操作任务上进行评估，并进行了4个真实世界实验验证。仿真结果显示，E3Flow在平均成功率上比最先进的球面扩散策略（SDP）提高了3.12%，同时实现了7倍的推理加速。

Conclusion: E3Flow为机器人策略学习展示了性能、效率和数据效率之间新的高度有效的权衡，为等变方法在实际应用中的部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: While existing equivariant methods enhance data efficiency, they suffer from high computational intensity, reliance on single-modality inputs, and instability when combined with fast-sampling methods. In this work, we propose E3Flow, a novel framework that addresses the critical limitations of equivariant diffusion policies. E3Flow overcomes these challenges, successfully unifying efficient rectified flow with stable, multi-modal equivariant learning for the first time. Our framework is built upon spherical harmonic representations to ensure rigorous SO(3) equivariance. We introduce a novel invariant Feature Enhancement Module (FEM) that dynamically fuses hybrid visual modalities (point clouds and images), injecting rich visual cues into the spherical harmonic features. We evaluate E3Flow on 8 manipulation tasks from the MimicGen and further conduct 4 real-world experiments to validate its effectiveness in physical environments. Simulation results show that E3Flow achieves a 3.12% improvement in average success rate over the state-of-the-art Spherical Diffusion Policy (SDP) while simultaneously delivering a 7x inference speedup. E3Flow thus demonstrates a new and highly effective trade-off between performance, efficiency, and data efficiency for robotic policy learning. Code: https://github.com/zql-kk/E3Flow.

</details>


### [166] [Learning Multi-Agent Local Collision-Avoidance for Collaborative Carrying tasks with Coupled Quadrupedal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23278)
*Francesca Bray,Simone Tolomei,Andrei Cramariuc,Cesar Cadena,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于强化学习的感知式分层控制策略，使两个四足机器人能够协同搬运物体，在未知环境中仅使用机载传感器实现避障和速度跟踪，无需预计算轨迹或完整地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人协同搬运在仓库和建筑工地等场景有重要应用价值，但多机器人协调运动面临挑战。现有方法主要针对无障碍环境，或依赖特定地形配置和预记录地图，难以适应真实世界的未知环境。

Method: 提出基于强化学习的分层架构：感知式高层物体中心策略控制两个预训练的运动策略；采用游戏启发的渐进式课程学习增加地形障碍复杂度；通过球形关节连接两个四足机器人搬运物体。

Result: 在硬件实验中验证了系统在未知环境中运动的能力，无需地图或路径规划器；与基于优化的方法和去中心化RL基线相比表现更优；成功实现了仅使用机载传感器的避障和速度跟踪。

Conclusion: 提出的RL分层控制策略能够使两个四足机器人协同搬运物体，在未知环境中实现避障和速度跟踪，无需预计算轨迹或完整地图知识，为真实世界应用提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: Robotic collaborative carrying could greatly benefit human activities like warehouse and construction site management. However, coordinating the simultaneous motion of multiple robots represents a significant challenge. Existing works primarily focus on obstacle-free environments, making them unsuitable for most real-world applications. Works that account for obstacles, either overfit to a specific terrain configuration or rely on pre-recorded maps combined with path planners to compute collision-free trajectories. This work focuses on two quadrupedal robots mechanically connected to a carried object. We propose a Reinforcement Learning (RL)-based policy that enables tracking a commanded velocity direction while avoiding collisions with nearby obstacles using only onboard sensing, eliminating the need for precomputed trajectories and complete map knowledge. Our work presents a hierarchical architecture, where a perceptive high-level object-centric policy commands two pretrained locomotion policies. Additionally, we employ a game-inspired curriculum to increase the complexity of obstacles in the terrain progressively. We validate our approach on two quadrupedal robots connected to a bar via spherical joints, benchmarking it against optimization-based and decentralized RL baselines. Our hardware experiments demonstrate the ability of our system to locomote in unknown environments without the need for a map or a path planner. The video of our work is available in the multimedia material.

</details>


### [167] [Strain-Parameterized Coupled Dynamics and Dual-Camera Visual Servoing for Aerial Continuum Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23333)
*Niloufar Amiri,Farrokh Janabi-Sharifi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对肌腱驱动空中连续体机械臂(TD-ACM)的广义动力学建模方法，并开发了鲁棒的双摄像头视觉伺服控制方案，解决了现有方法计算成本高、未考虑平台欠驱动等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TD-ACM的耦合动力学建模方法计算成本高，且未明确考虑空中平台的欠驱动特性，限制了实际应用效果。

Method: 1) 将应变参数化Cosserat杆模型与无人机刚体模型集成到统一的SE(3)拉格朗日常微分方程框架中；2) 基于该模型开发鲁棒的双摄像头图像视觉伺服方案，解决视野限制、姿态引起的图像运动问题；3) 集成低层自适应控制器处理建模不确定性。

Result: 通过大量仿真和紧凑型定制原型的实验验证，证明了所提框架在实际场景中的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出的广义动力学建模方法和鲁棒视觉伺服控制方案成功解决了TD-ACM的建模和控制挑战，为实际应用提供了可靠的理论框架。

Abstract: Tendon-driven aerial continuum manipulators (TD-ACMs) combine the maneuverability of uncrewed aerial vehicles (UAVs) with the compliance of lightweight continuum robots (CRs). Existing coupled dynamic modeling approaches for TD-ACMs incur high computational costs and do not explicitly account for aerial platform underactuation. To address these limitations, this paper presents a generalized dynamic formulation of a coupled TD-ACM with an underactuated base. The proposed approach integrates a strain-parameterized Cosserat rod model with a rigid-body model of the UAV into a unified Lagrangian ordinary differential equation (ODE) framework on $\mathrm{SE}(3)$, thereby eliminating computationally intensive symbolic derivations. Building upon the developed model, a robust dual-camera image-based visual servoing (IBVS) scheme is introduced. The proposed controller mitigates the field-of-view (FoV) limitations of conventional IBVS, compensates for attitude-induced image motion caused by UAV lateral dynamics, and incorporates a low-level adaptive controller to address modeling uncertainties with formal stability guarantees. Extensive simulations and experimental validation on a compact custom-built prototype demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed framework in real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [168] [Edge Radar Material Classification Under Geometry Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23342)
*Jannik Hohmann,Dong Wang,Andreas Nüchter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于毫米波雷达的材料分类方法在几何变化下性能显著下降，需要改进鲁棒性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料感知能提升机器人导航和交互能力，特别是在摄像头和激光雷达性能下降的环境中。需要为超低功耗边缘设备开发轻量级毫米波雷达材料分类方案。

Method: 使用紧凑的距离-强度描述符和多层感知机(MLP)构建轻量级毫米波雷达材料分类流水线，针对TI IWRL6432等超低功耗边缘设备进行优化，实现实时推理。

Result: 在标准训练几何条件下，分类器达到94.2%的宏F1分数；但在实际几何变化（传感器高度变化和小倾斜角）下，性能显著下降至约68.5%。这些扰动引起系统性强度缩放和角度相关的雷达散射截面效应，导致特征分布偏移。

Conclusion: 毫米波雷达材料分类在几何变化下存在鲁棒性问题，需要采用归一化、几何增强和运动感知特征等方法来提高系统在实际应用中的稳定性。

Abstract: Material awareness can improve robotic navigation and interaction, particularly in conditions where cameras and LiDAR degrade. We present a lightweight mmWave radar material classification pipeline designed for ultra-low-power edge devices (TI IWRL6432), using compact range-bin intensity descriptors and a Multilayer Perceptron (MLP) for real-time inference. While the classifier reaches a macro-F1 of 94.2\% under the nominal training geometry, we observe a pronounced performance drop under realistic geometry shifts, including sensor height changes and small tilt angles. These perturbations induce systematic intensity scaling and angle-dependent radar cross section (RCS) effects, pushing features out of distribution and reducing macro-F1 to around 68.5\%. We analyze these failure modes and outline practical directions for improving robustness with normalization, geometry augmentation, and motion-aware features.

</details>


### [169] [PinPoint: Monocular Needle Pose Estimation for Robotic Suturing via Stein Variational Newton and Geometric Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23365)
*Jesse F. d'Almeida,Tanner Watts,Susheela Sharma Stern,James Ferguson,Alan Kuntz,Robert J. Webster*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PinPoint：一种用于单目内窥镜下手术针姿态估计的概率变分推理框架，通过保持多模态假设分布而非抑制模糊性，显著提升了姿态估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目内窥镜手术中，由于深度模糊性和旋转对称性，手术针的3D姿态估计本质上是不适定问题，产生多模态可行配置分布而非单一可靠估计。现有方法主要在立体视觉下工作，无法有效处理单目环境下的模糊性。

Method: PinPoint采用概率变分推理框架，结合单目图像观测与机器人抓取约束，通过解析几何似然函数和闭式雅可比矩阵。使用Stein变分牛顿推理，其中二阶粒子传输确定性地将粒子移向高概率区域，同时基于核的排斥力保持多模态结构的多样性。

Result: 在真实针跟踪序列中，PinPoint相比粒子滤波基线将平均平移误差降低80%（降至1.00 mm），旋转误差降低78%（降至13.80°），不确定性校准更好。在诱导旋转序列中，PinPoint保持双峰后验的概率为84%，几乎是基线方法的三倍。

Conclusion: PinPoint通过直接处理单目姿态估计的模糊性，保持多模态假设分布而非过早收敛到单一模式，在单目内窥镜手术中实现了可靠的手术针姿态跟踪，即使在针完全嵌入组织时也能保持稳定跟踪。

Abstract: Reliable estimation of surgical needle 3D position and orientation is essential for autonomous robotic suturing, yet existing methods operate almost exclusively under stereoscopic vision. In monocular endoscopic settings, common in transendoscopic and intraluminal procedures, depth ambiguity and rotational symmetry render needle pose estimation inherently ill-posed, producing a multimodal distribution over feasible configurations, rather than a single, well-grounded estimate. We present PinPoint, a probabilistic variational inference framework that treats this ambiguity directly, maintaining a distribution of pose hypotheses rather than suppressing it. PinPoint combines monocular image observations with robot-grasp constraints through analytical geometric likelihoods with closed-form Jacobians. This framework enables efficient Gauss-Newton preconditioning in a Stein Variational Newton inference, where second-order particle transport deterministically moves particles toward high-probability regions while kernel-based repulsion preserves diversity in the multimodal structure. On real needle-tracking sequences, PinPoint reduces mean translational error by 80% (down to 1.00 mm) and rotational error by 78% (down to 13.80°) relative to a particle-filter baseline, with substantially better-calibrated uncertainty. On induced-rotation sequences, where monocular ambiguity is most severe, PinPoint maintains a bimodal posterior 84% of the time, almost three times the rate of the particle filter baseline, correctly preserving the alternative hypothesis rather than committing prematurely to one mode. Suturing experiments in ex vivo tissue demonstrate stable tracking through intermittent occlusion, with average errors during occlusion of 1.34 mm in translation and 19.18° in rotation, even when the needle is fully embedded.

</details>


### [170] [Rectify, Don't Regret: Avoiding Pitfalls of Differentiable Simulation in Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23393)
*Harsh Yadav,Christian Bohn,Tobias Meisen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种分离式滚动时域推演方法，解决自动驾驶轨迹预测中闭环可微分模拟器的捷径学习问题，通过切断计算图迫使模型学习真正的恢复行为而非非因果地优化过去预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前开环轨迹模型在现实自动驾驶中面临初始偏差累积问题，而完全可微分的闭环模拟器存在捷径学习缺陷：损失梯度通过诱导状态输入反向传播，泄漏未来真实信息到模型先前预测中，导致模型非因果地"后悔"过去错误而非学习真正的反应性恢复。

Method: 引入分离式滚动时域推演方法，通过在模拟步骤间显式切断计算图，使模型从漂移状态学习真正的恢复行为，强制其"纠正"错误而非非因果地优化过去预测。

Result: 在nuScenes和DeepScenario数据集上的广泛评估表明，该方法在高重规划频率下比完全可微分闭环训练减少目标碰撞达33.24%。相比标准开环基线，在密集环境中减少碰撞达27.74%，同时提高多模态预测多样性和车道对齐。

Conclusion: 分离式滚动时域推演方法有效解决了闭环可微分模拟器的捷径学习问题，使自动驾驶轨迹预测模型能够学习真正的恢复行为，显著提高在现实场景中的鲁棒性和安全性。

Abstract: Current open-loop trajectory models struggle in real-world autonomous driving because minor initial deviations often cascade into compounding errors, pushing the agent into out-of-distribution states. While fully differentiable closed-loop simulators attempt to address this, they suffer from shortcut learning: the loss gradients flow backward through induced state inputs, inadvertently leaking future ground truth information directly into the model's own previous predictions. The model exploits these signals to artificially avoid drift, non-causally "regretting" past mistakes rather than learning genuinely reactive recovery. To address this, we introduce a detached receding horizon rollout. By explicitly severing the computation graph between simulation steps, the model learns genuine recovery behaviors from drifted states, forcing it to "rectify" mistakes rather than non-causally optimizing past predictions. Extensive evaluations on the nuScenes and DeepScenario datasets show our approach yields more robust recovery strategies, reducing target collisions by up to 33.24% compared to fully differentiable closed-loop training at high replanning frequencies. Furthermore, compared to standard open-loop baselines, our non-differentiable framework decreases collisions by up to 27.74% in dense environments while simultaneously improving multi-modal prediction diversity and lane alignment.

</details>


### [171] [VTAM: Video-Tactile-Action Models for Complex Physical Interaction Beyond VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23481)
*Haoran Yuan,Weigang Yi,Zhenyu Zhang,Wendi Chen,Yuchen Mo,Jiashi Yin,Xinzhuo Li,Xiangyu Zeng,Chuan Wen,Cewu Lu,Katherine Driggs-Campbell,Ismini Lourentzou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VTAM是一种结合视觉与触觉的多模态世界建模框架，通过轻量级模态迁移微调增强预训练视频变换器，在接触丰富的操作任务中显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频-动作模型在接触丰富的场景中存在局限性，因为精细的力调节和接触转换无法仅从视觉信息可靠编码，导致行为不稳定或不精确。需要触觉感知作为补充的接地信号来弥补这一差距。

Method: VTAM通过轻量级模态迁移微调将触觉流集成到预训练视频变换器中，无需触觉-语言配对数据或独立的触觉预训练。引入触觉正则化损失来稳定多模态融合，防止视觉潜在主导动作模型。

Result: VTAM在接触丰富的操作任务中保持90%的平均稳健成功率。在需要高保真力感知的挑战性场景（如薯片拾取放置）中，比π0.5基线提升80%的性能。

Conclusion: 集成触觉反馈对于纠正世界动作模型中的视觉估计误差至关重要，为物理接地的具身基础模型提供了可扩展的方法。

Abstract: Video-Action Models (VAMs) have emerged as a promising framework for embodied intelligence, learning implicit world dynamics from raw video streams to produce temporally consistent action predictions. Although such models demonstrate strong performance on long-horizon tasks through visual reasoning, they remain limited in contact-rich scenarios where critical interaction states are only partially observable from vision alone. In particular, fine-grained force modulation and contact transitions are not reliably encoded in visual tokens, leading to unstable or imprecise behaviors. To bridge this gap, we introduce the Video-Tactile Action Model (VTAM), a multimodal world modeling framework that incorporates tactile perception as a complementary grounding signal. VTAM augments a pretrained video transformer with tactile streams via a lightweight modality transfer finetuning, enabling efficient cross-modal representation learning without tactile-language paired data or independent tactile pretraining. To stabilize multimodal fusion, we introduce a tactile regularization loss that enforces balanced cross-modal attention, preventing visual latent dominance in the action model. VTAM demonstrates superior performance in contact-rich manipulation, maintaining a robust success rate of 90 percent on average. In challenging scenarios such as potato chip pick-and-place requiring high-fidelity force awareness, VTAM outperforms the pi 0.5 baseline by 80 percent. Our findings demonstrate that integrating tactile feedback is essential for correcting visual estimation errors in world action models, providing a scalable approach to physically grounded embodied foundation models.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [Memory Bear AI Memory Science Engine for Multimodal Affective Intelligence: A Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22306)
*Deliang Wen,Ke Sun,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Memory Bear AI Memory Science Engine，一种基于记忆的多模态情感智能框架，将情感信息建模为记忆系统中的结构化变量，而非瞬时输出标签，以解决长期依赖、不完整输入和持续情感记忆等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实交互中的情感判断很少是纯粹的局部预测问题，情感意义通常依赖于先前的轨迹、累积的上下文以及可能微弱、嘈杂或不完整的多模态证据。现有多模态情感识别系统大多优化于短程推理，对持续情感记忆、长时程依赖建模和不完美输入下的鲁棒解释支持有限。

Method: 提出记忆中心的多模态情感智能框架，将情感信息建模为记忆系统中的结构化演化变量。通过结构化记忆形成、工作记忆聚合、长期巩固、记忆驱动检索、动态融合校准和持续记忆更新等过程组织处理。核心是将多模态信号转换为结构化情感记忆单元，使情感信息能在交互过程中被保存、重新激活和修订。

Result: 实验结果显示，在基准测试和实际业务场景中，该框架相比对比系统均取得一致性能提升，具有更强的准确性和鲁棒性，特别是在噪声或缺失模态条件下表现更优。

Conclusion: 该框架是从局部情感识别向更连续、鲁棒且部署相关的多模态情感智能迈出的实用一步，通过记忆系统建模情感信息，解决了现有系统在长期依赖、不完整输入和持续记忆方面的局限性。

Abstract: Affective judgment in real interaction is rarely a purely local prediction problem. Emotional meaning often depends on prior trajectory, accumulated context, and multimodal evidence that may be weak, noisy, or incomplete at the current moment. Although multimodal emotion recognition (MER) has improved the integration of text, speech, and visual signals, many existing systems remain optimized for short-range inference and provide limited support for persistent affective memory, long-horizon dependency modeling, and robust interpretation under imperfect input.
  This technical report presents the Memory Bear AI Memory Science Engine, a memory-centered framework for multimodal affective intelligence. Instead of treating emotion as a transient output label, the framework models affective information as a structured and evolving variable within a memory system. It organizes processing through structured memory formation, working-memory aggregation, long-term consolidation, memory-driven retrieval, dynamic fusion calibration, and continuous memory updating. At its core, multimodal signals are transformed into structured Emotion Memory Units (EMUs), enabling affective information to be preserved, reactivated, and revised across interaction horizons.
  Experimental results show consistent gains over comparison systems across benchmark and business-grounded settings, with stronger accuracy and robustness, especially under noisy or missing-modality conditions. The framework offers a practical step from local emotion recognition toward more continuous, robust, and deployment-relevant affective intelligence.

</details>


### [173] [The Efficiency Attenuation Phenomenon: A Computational Challenge to the Language of Thought Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22312)
*Di Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过计算实验挑战"思维语言假说"，发现AI智能体通过强化学习发展出的非符号化通信协议比人类可理解的符号化协议效率高50.5%，支持认知架构多元主义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是验证思维是否需要类似语言的格式（思维语言假说），通过AI私密语言思想实验来检验符号化思维是否是认知协作的最优形式。

Method: 采用多智能体强化学习框架，在部分可观察的协作导航任务中，让两个AI智能体发展出内生的通信协议，并与预定义的人类可理解符号化协议进行性能对比。

Result: 结果显示，使用内生协议的智能体比使用预定义符号化协议的智能体效率高出50.5%，证实了效率衰减现象，表明最优协作认知并非由符号结构介导。

Conclusion: 研究支持认知架构多元主义，认为最优协作认知自然耦合于亚符号计算而非符号结构，对AI伦理和认知科学有重要启示。

Abstract: This paper computationally investigates whether thought requires a language-like format, as posited by the Language of Thought (LoT) hypothesis. We introduce the ``AI Private Language'' thought experiment: if two artificial agents develop an efficient, inscrutable communication protocol via multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), and their performance declines when forced to use a human-comprehensible language, this Efficiency Attenuation Phenomenon (EAP) challenges the LoT. We formalize this in a cooperative navigation task under partial observability. Results show that agents with an emergent protocol achieve 50.5\% higher efficiency than those using a pre-defined, human-like symbolic protocol, confirming the EAP. This suggests optimal collaborative cognition in these systems is not mediated by symbolic structures but is naturally coupled with sub-symbolic computations. The work bridges philosophy, cognitive science, and AI, arguing for pluralism in cognitive architectures and highlighting implications for AI ethics.

</details>


### [174] [Session Risk Memory (SRM): Temporal Authorization for Deterministic Pre-Execution Safety Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22350)
*Florin Adrian Chitan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SRM扩展了确定性预执行安全门，通过轨迹级授权检测分布式攻击，在保持100%检测率的同时消除误报，单轮开销低于250微秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有确定性预执行安全门虽然能有效评估单步动作授权，但无法检测将有害意图分解为多个合规步骤的分布式攻击。需要一种轻量级方法扩展状态执行门，实现轨迹级授权。

Method: 提出Session Risk Memory (SRM)，一个轻量级确定性模块，通过维护会话行为特征的紧凑语义质心，并对基线调整后的门输出进行指数移动平均来累积风险信号。SRM使用与底层门相同的语义向量表示，无需额外模型组件、训练或概率推理。

Result: 在包含慢速数据泄露、渐进权限提升和合规性漂移场景的80个多轮会话基准测试中，ILION+SRM达到F1=1.0000且误报率为0%，而无状态ILION的F1=0.9756且误报率为5%。两者检测率均为100%，SRM单轮开销低于250微秒。

Conclusion: SRM框架引入了空间授权一致性（按动作评估）和时间授权一致性（按轨迹评估）的概念区分，为智能体系统提供了会话级安全的理论基础，有效解决了分布式攻击检测问题。

Abstract: Deterministic pre-execution safety gates evaluate whether individual agent actions are compatible with their assigned roles. While effective at per-action authorization, these systems are structurally blind to distributed attacks that decompose harmful intent across multiple individually-compliant steps. This paper introduces Session Risk Memory (SRM), a lightweight deterministic module that extends stateless execution gates with trajectory-level authorization. SRM maintains a compact semantic centroid representing the evolving behavioral profile of an agent session and accumulates a risk signal through exponential moving average over baseline-subtracted gate outputs. It operates on the same semantic vector representation as the underlying gate, requiring no additional model components, training, or probabilistic inference. We evaluate SRM on a multi-turn benchmark of 80 sessions containing slow-burn exfiltration, gradual privilege escalation, and compliance drift scenarios. Results show that ILION+SRM achieves F1 = 1.0000 with 0% false positive rate, compared to stateless ILION at F1 = 0.9756 with 5% FPR, while maintaining 100% detection rate for both systems. Critically, SRM eliminates all false positives with a per-turn overhead under 250 microseconds. The framework introduces a conceptual distinction between spatial authorization consistency (evaluated per action) and temporal authorization consistency (evaluated over trajectory), providing a principled basis for session-level safety in agentic systems.

</details>


### [175] [From Static Templates to Dynamic Runtime Graphs: A Survey of Workflow Optimization for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22386)
*Ling Yue,Kushal Raj Bhandari,Ching-Yun Ko,Dhaval Patel,Shuxin Lin,Nianjun Zhou,Jianxi Gao,Pin-Yu Chen,Shaowu Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文综述了基于大语言模型(LLM)的智能体工作流优化方法，提出了"智能体计算图(ACG)"框架，按工作流结构确定时机进行分类，并建立了三维分析框架来系统化现有研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于LLM的系统通过构建可执行工作流来解决复杂任务的需求日益增长，需要系统化地整理和分类现有的工作流设计和优化方法，为研究者提供清晰的术语、统一的分析框架和可比较的评估标准。

Method: 提出智能体计算图(ACG)概念框架，按工作流结构确定时机将方法分为静态和动态两类；建立三维分析框架：何时确定结构、优化工作流的哪个部分、使用何种评估信号；区分可重用模板、运行特定图和执行轨迹三个层次。

Result: 建立了系统化的分类框架，将现有文献按结构确定时机、优化对象和评估信号三个维度进行组织；提出了结构感知的评估视角，补充了传统任务指标；提供了统一的术语和可比较的分析框架。

Conclusion: 该综述为LLM智能体工作流优化研究提供了清晰的词汇表、统一的方法定位框架、更可比较的文献视角和更可复现的评估标准，有助于推动该领域的系统化发展。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based systems are becoming increasingly popular for solving tasks by constructing executable workflows that interleave LLM calls, information retrieval, tool use, code execution, memory updates, and verification. This survey reviews recent methods for designing and optimizing such workflows, which we treat as agentic computation graphs (ACGs). We organize the literature based on when workflow structure is determined, where structure refers to which components or agents are present, how they depend on each other, and how information flows between them. This lens distinguishes static methods, which fix a reusable workflow scaffold before deployment, from dynamic methods, which select, generate, or revise the workflow for a particular run before or during execution. We further organize prior work along three dimensions: when structure is determined, what part of the workflow is optimized, and which evaluation signals guide optimization (e.g., task metrics, verifier signals, preferences, or trace-derived feedback). We also distinguish reusable workflow templates, run-specific realized graphs, and execution traces, separating reusable design choices from the structures actually deployed in a given run and from realized runtime behavior. Finally, we outline a structure-aware evaluation perspective that complements downstream task metrics with graph-level properties, execution cost, robustness, and structural variation across inputs. Our goal is to provide a clear vocabulary, a unified framework for positioning new methods, a more comparable view of existing body of literature, and a more reproducible evaluation standard for future work in workflow optimizations for LLM agents.

</details>


### [176] [Computational Arbitrage in AI Model Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22404)
*Ricardo Olmedo,Bernhard Schölkopf,Moritz Hardt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AI模型市场中套利策略的研究：通过在不同模型提供商间分配推理预算，套利者能以更低成本提供解决方案，获得高达40%的净利润率，影响市场定价和竞争格局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究AI模型市场中套利行为的动机在于探索竞争性模型提供商市场中，如何通过资源优化配置来创造价值。当前市场存在不同成本和能力的模型提供商，客户愿意为可验证的解决方案支付预算，这为套利创造了机会。套利者可以通过跨提供商分配推理预算来降低市场报价，同时避免模型开发风险。

Method: 采用实证研究方法，通过SWE-bench GitHub问题解决案例研究，使用GPT-5 mini和DeepSeek v3.2两个代表性模型进行验证。在可验证领域测试简单套利策略，并探索跨领域鲁棒套利策略。研究还分析了蒸馏技术对套利机会的影响，以及多个套利者竞争对市场的影响。

Result: 在可验证领域（如代码问题解决），简单套利策略能产生高达40%的净利润率。鲁棒套利策略在不同领域仍能保持盈利。蒸馏技术创造了强大的套利机会，但可能损害教师模型的收入。多个竞争套利者会降低消费者价格，减少模型提供商的边际收入。同时，套利减少了市场分割，使小型模型提供商能够通过早期收入获取进入市场。

Conclusion: 套利是AI模型市场中一个强大的力量，对模型开发、蒸馏和部署具有重要影响。套利策略能够创造显著的经济价值，同时改变市场动态：降低消费者价格、减少模型提供商利润，但促进市场整合和降低进入壁垒，为小型提供商创造机会。这一现象需要在模型市场设计和监管中加以考虑。

Abstract: Consider a market of competing model providers selling query access to models with varying costs and capabilities. Customers submit problem instances and are willing to pay up to a budget for a verifiable solution. An arbitrageur efficiently allocates inference budget across providers to undercut the market, thus creating a competitive offering with no model-development risk. In this work, we initiate the study of arbitrage in AI model markets, empirically demonstrating the viability of arbitrage and illustrating its economic consequences. We conduct an in-depth case study of SWE-bench GitHub issue resolution using two representative models, GPT-5 mini and DeepSeek v3.2. In this verifiable domain, simple arbitrage strategies generate net profit margins of up to 40%. Robust arbitrage strategies that generalize across different domains remain profitable. Distillation further creates strong arbitrage opportunities, potentially at the expense of the teacher model's revenue. Multiple competing arbitrageurs drive down consumer prices, reducing the marginal revenue of model providers. At the same time, arbitrage reduces market segmentation and facilitates market entry for smaller model providers by enabling earlier revenue capture. Our results suggest that arbitrage can be a powerful force in AI model markets with implications for model development, distillation, and deployment.

</details>


### [177] [AI Mental Models: Learned Intuition and Deliberation in a Bounded Neural Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22561)
*Laurence Anthony*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了有限神经架构能否在经典64项三段论推理基准上实现直觉与审慎的有意义分工，发现双路径架构中审慎路径比直觉路径表现更好，并显示出类似推理的内部结构组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探索有限神经架构能否在经典三段论推理任务中实现直觉与审慎的认知分工，为AI中的世界模型和多阶段推理辩论提供受控测试环境，检验学习系统是否能发展结构化内部计算而非仅依赖一次性关联预测。

Method: 实验1评估了直接神经基线在5折交叉验证下预测9类人类响应分布的能力。实验2引入了有限双路径架构，包含独立的直觉和审慎路径，基于计算心理模型理论。通过交叉验证、80:20可解释性运行和五种子稳定性扫描来分析性能差异和内部结构。

Result: 有限直觉路径的聚合相关性为r=0.7272，有限审慎路径为r=0.8152，审慎优势在交叉验证中显著（p=0.0101）。最大留出增益出现在NVC、Eca和Oca类别，表明对拒绝响应和c-a结论的处理改善。可解释性运行显示审慎路径发展出稀疏、分化的内部结构，包括Oac倾向状态、主导工作状态和几个弱使用或未使用状态。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在有限条件下，双路径架构能够发展出类似推理的内部组织，审慎路径比直觉路径表现更好且具有更结构化的内部状态。但研究并未声称模型完全复制了模型构建、反例搜索和结论修订的完整顺序过程。

Abstract: This paper asks whether a bounded neural architecture can exhibit a meaningful division of labor between intuition and deliberation on a classic 64-item syllogistic reasoning benchmark. More broadly, the benchmark is relevant to ongoing debates about world models and multi-stage reasoning in AI. It provides a controlled setting for testing whether a learned system can develop structured internal computation rather than only one-shot associative prediction. Experiment 1 evaluates a direct neural baseline for predicting full 9-way human response distributions under 5-fold cross-validation. Experiment 2 introduces a bounded dual-path architecture with separate intuition and deliberation pathways, motivated by computational mental-model theory (Khemlani & Johnson-Laird, 2022). Under cross-validation, bounded intuition reaches an aggregate correlation of r = 0.7272, whereas bounded deliberation reaches r = 0.8152, and the deliberation advantage is significant across folds (p = 0.0101). The largest held-out gains occur for NVC, Eca, and Oca, suggesting improved handling of rejection responses and c-a conclusions. A canonical 80:20 interpretability run and a five-seed stability sweep further indicate that the deliberation pathway develops sparse, differentiated internal structure, including an Oac-leaning state, a dominant workhorse state, and several weakly used or unused states whose exact indices vary across runs. These findings are consistent with reasoning-like internal organization under bounded conditions, while stopping short of any claim that the model reproduces full sequential processes of model construction, counterexample search, and conclusion revision.

</details>


### [178] [Understanding LLM Performance Degradation in Multi-Instance Processing: The Roles of Instance Count and Context Length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22608)
*Jingxuan Chen,Mohammad Taher Pilehvar,Jose Camacho-Collados*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLMs在多实例处理任务中表现出性能下降模式：小实例数（约20-100）时轻微下降，大实例数时性能崩溃；实例数量比上下文长度对性能影响更大


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LLM在单个任务上表现优异，但缺乏对多实例输入处理能力的研究。用户经常依赖LLM处理多个文档或分析多个实例，需要了解LLM在多实例处理中的表现

Method: 对LLM在多实例处理能力进行综合评估，测试其在擅长任务上的表现，分析实例数量和上下文长度对性能的影响

Result: 所有LLM都呈现相似模式：小实例数（约20-100）时性能轻微下降，大实例数时性能崩溃。实例数量比上下文长度对最终结果影响更大

Conclusion: 优化LLM多实例处理性能时，应同时关注上下文长度和实例数量，特别是实例数量对性能有更强影响

Abstract: Users often rely on Large Language Models (LLMs) for processing multiple documents or performing analysis over a number of instances. For example, analysing the overall sentiment of a number of movie reviews requires an LLM to process the sentiment of each review individually in order to provide a final aggregated answer. While LLM performance on such individual tasks is generally high, there has been little research on how LLMs perform when dealing with multi-instance inputs. In this paper, we perform a comprehensive evaluation of the multi-instance processing (MIP) ability of LLMs for tasks in which they excel individually. The results show that all LLMs follow a pattern of slight performance degradation for small numbers of instances (approximately 20-100), followed by a performance collapse on larger instance counts. Crucially, our analysis shows that while context length is associated with this degradation, the number of instances has a stronger effect on the final results. This finding suggests that when optimising LLM performance for MIP, attention should be paid to both context length and, in particular, instance count.

</details>


### [179] [Graph-Aware Late Chunking for Retrieval-Augmented Generation in Biomedical Literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22633)
*Pouria Mortezaagha,Arya Rahgozar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GraLC-RAG框架通过图感知结构智能统一了延迟分块，解决了生物医学文献RAG系统中标准排名指标忽略检索广度的问题，展示了结构感知方法在跨章节证据检索方面的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生物医学文献检索增强生成系统主要使用MRR等排名指标进行评估，这些指标只关注检索精度而忽略了检索广度，无法评估系统从文档不同结构部分检索证据的能力，这对于全文科学文档的合成任务是不完整的。

Method: 提出GraLC-RAG框架，统一延迟分块与图感知结构智能，包括结构感知分块边界检测、UMLS知识图谱注入和图引导混合检索。在2,359篇IMRaD格式的PubMed Central文章上评估了六种策略，使用2,033个跨章节问题和两种指标家族：标准排名指标和结构覆盖指标。

Result: 内容相似性方法获得最高MRR（0.517）但总是从单个章节检索，而结构感知方法能从多达15.6倍更多的章节检索证据。知识图谱注入检索将答案质量差距缩小到delta-F1=0.009，同时保持4.6倍的章节多样性。

Conclusion: 标准指标系统性地低估了结构检索的价值，缩小多章节合成差距是生物医学RAG的关键开放问题。结构感知方法在证据检索广度方面具有显著优势，而知识图谱注入能在保持答案质量的同时提高检索多样性。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems for biomedical literature are typically evaluated using ranking metrics like Mean Reciprocal Rank (MRR), which measure how well the system identifies the single most relevant chunk. We argue that for full-text scientific documents, this paradigm is incomplete: it rewards retrieval precision while ignoring retrieval breadth -- the ability to surface evidence from across a document's structural sections. We propose GraLC-RAG, a framework that unifies late chunking with graph-aware structural intelligence, introducing structure-aware chunk boundary detection, UMLS knowledge graph infusion, and graph-guided hybrid retrieval. We evaluate six strategies on 2,359 IMRaD-filtered PubMed Central articles using 2,033 cross-section questions and two metric families: standard ranking metrics (MRR, Recall@k) and structural coverage metrics (SecCov@k, CS Recall). Our results expose a sharp divergence: content-similarity methods achieve the highest MRR (0.517) but always retrieve from a single section, while structure-aware methods retrieve from up to 15.6x more sections. Generation experiments show that KG-infused retrieval narrows the answer-quality gap to delta-F1 = 0.009 while maintaining 4.6x section diversity. These findings demonstrate that standard metrics systematically undervalue structural retrieval and that closing the multi-section synthesis gap is a key open problem for biomedical RAG.

</details>


### [180] [Benchmarking Multi-Agent LLM Architectures for Financial Document Processing: A Comparative Study of Orchestration Patterns, Cost-Accuracy Tradeoffs and Production Scaling Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22651)
*Siddhant Kulkarni,Yukta Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 系统评估四种多智能体编排架构在金融文档信息抽取中的性能，发现自反式架构准确率最高但成本也最高，分层架构在成本-准确率帕累托前沿表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在金融文档结构化信息抽取中的应用迅速增长，但生产部署面临架构选择缺乏实证指导的问题。需要系统比较不同多智能体编排架构的性能，为实际部署提供依据。

Method: 系统比较四种多智能体编排架构：顺序流水线、并行扇出合并、分层监督-工作者和自反式自校正循环。在10,000份SEC文件（10-K、10-Q和8-K表格）上评估五种前沿和开源LLM，涵盖25个抽取字段类型，从五个维度衡量：字段级F1、文档级准确率、端到端延迟、每文档成本和令牌效率。

Result: 自反式架构获得最高字段级F1（0.943），但成本是顺序基线的2.3倍；分层架构在成本-准确率帕累托前沿表现最佳（F1 0.921，成本1.4倍）。混合配置可恢复自反式架构89%的准确率提升，成本仅增加15%。从1K到100K文档/天的扩展分析揭示了吞吐量-准确率退化曲线。

Conclusion: 研究为在受监管金融环境中部署多智能体LLM系统提供了可操作的指导，分层架构在成本效益方面表现最佳，混合配置可在成本和准确率间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: The adoption of large language models (LLMs) for structured information extraction from financial documents has accelerated rapidly, yet production deployments face fundamental architectural decisions with limited empirical guidance. We present a systematic benchmark comparing four multi-agent orchestration architectures: sequential pipeline, parallel fan-out with merge, hierarchical supervisor-worker and reflexive self-correcting loop. These are evaluated across five frontier and open-weight LLMs on a corpus of 10,000 SEC filings (10-K, 10-Q and 8-K forms). Our evaluation spans 25 extraction field types covering governance structures, executive compensation and financial metrics, measured along five axes: field-level F1, document-level accuracy, end-to-end latency, cost per document and token efficiency. We find that reflexive architectures achieve the highest field-level F1 (0.943) but at 2.3x the cost of sequential baselines, while hierarchical architectures occupy the most favorable position on the cost-accuracy Pareto frontier (F1 0.921 at 1.4x cost). We further present ablation studies on semantic caching, model routing and adaptive retry strategies, demonstrating that hybrid configurations can recover 89\% of the reflexive architecture's accuracy gains at only 1.15x baseline cost. Our scaling analysis from 1K to 100K documents per day reveals non-obvious throughput-accuracy degradation curves that inform capacity planning. These findings provide actionable guidance for practitioners deploying multi-agent LLM systems in regulated financial environments.

</details>


### [181] [MuQ-Eval: An Open-Source Per-Sample Quality Metric for AI Music Generation Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22677)
*Di Zhu,Zixuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MUQ-EVAL是一个开源的AI生成音乐质量评估指标，通过在冻结的MuQ-310M特征上训练轻量级预测头构建，在系统级和话语级均与人类评分高度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布性指标（如Fréchet Audio Distance）无法对单个音乐片段评分且与人类判断相关性差，而唯一实现高人类相关性的逐样本学习指标是闭源的，因此需要开发开源的高质量逐样本音乐质量评估指标。

Method: 使用MusicEval数据集（包含31个文本到音乐系统生成的片段及专家质量评分），在冻结的MuQ-310M特征上训练轻量级预测头。最简单模型采用冻结特征、注意力池化和两层MLP。系统研究了训练目标和适应策略的消融实验。

Result: 最简单模型在系统级SRCC=0.957，话语级SRCC=0.838。冻结基线模型表现最佳，表明冻结MuQ表示已捕获质量相关信息。编码器选择是主导设计因素。LoRA适应模型仅需150个片段即可获得可用相关性。对信号级伪影敏感但对音乐结构失真不敏感。

Conclusion: MUQ-EVAL是完全开源的逐样本质量评估指标，优于现有开源指标，可在单个消费级GPU上实时运行。冻结预训练表示足以构建高质量音乐评估器，支持从个体听众标注创建个性化质量评估器。

Abstract: Distributional metrics such as Fréchet Audio Distance cannot score individual music clips and correlate poorly with human judgments, while the only per-sample learned metric achieving high human correlation is closed-source. We introduce MUQ-EVAL, an open-source per-sample quality metric for AIgenerated music built by training lightweight prediction heads on frozen MuQ-310M features using MusicEval, a dataset of generated clips from 31 text-to-music systems with expert quality ratings. Our simplest model, frozen features with attention pooling and a two-layer MLP, achieves system-level SRCC = 0.957 and utterance-level SRCC = 0.838 with human mean opinion scores. A systematic ablation over training objectives and adaptation strategies shows that no addition meaningfully improves the frozen baseline, indicating that frozen MuQ representations already capture quality-relevant information. Encoder choice is the dominant design factor, outweighing all architectural and training decisions. LoRA-adapted models trained on as few as 150 clips already achieve usable correlation, enabling personalized quality evaluators from individual listener annotations. A controlled degradation analysis reveals selective sensitivity to signal-level artifacts but insensitivity to musical-structural distortions. Our metric, MUQ-EVAL, is fully open-source, outperforms existing open per-sample metrics, and runs in real time on a single consumer GPU. Code, model weights, and evaluation scripts are available at https://github.com/dgtql/MuQ-Eval.

</details>


### [182] [HyFI: Hyperbolic Feature Interpolation for Brain-Vision Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22721)
*Sangmin Jo,Wootaek Jeong,Da-Woon Heo,Yoohwan Hwang,Heung-Il Suk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Hyperbolic Feature Interpolation (HyFI)框架，利用双曲空间特性解决脑信号与图像特征对齐中的模态鸿沟和特征纠缠问题，在脑-图像检索任务中取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法独立对齐脑信号与图像的语义和感知特征，但忽略了两个关键挑战：1) 脑信号与图像之间的模态鸿沟（信息量差异）；2) 语义和感知特征在神经活动中高度纠缠。需要一种能同时处理信息量差异和特征纠缠的框架。

Method: 提出Hyperbolic Feature Interpolation (HyFI)框架，利用双曲空间的几何特性：1) 适合处理信息量差异；2) 两点间测地线自然向原点弯曲（原点处表示能力较低）。沿双曲测地线在语义和感知视觉特征之间进行插值，实现感知和语义信息的融合与压缩，反映脑信号的有限表达能力及特征纠缠特性。

Result: 在零样本脑-图像检索任务中取得最先进性能：THINGS-EEG数据集Top-1准确率提升高达+17.3%，THINGS-MEG数据集提升+9.1%，显著优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 双曲特征插值框架能有效解决脑信号与视觉特征对齐中的模态鸿沟和特征纠缠问题，通过利用双曲空间的几何特性实现特征融合与压缩，显著提升脑-图像检索性能。

Abstract: Recent progress in artificial intelligence has encouraged numerous attempts to understand and decode human visual system from brain signals. These prior works typically align neural activity independently with semantic and perceptual features extracted from images using pre-trained vision models. However, they fail to account for two key challenges: (1) the modality gap arising from the natural difference in the information level of representation between brain signals and images, and (2) the fact that semantic and perceptual features are highly entangled within neural activity. To address these issues, we utilize hyperbolic space, which is well-suited for considering differences in the amount of information and has the geometric property that geodesics between two points naturally bend toward the origin, where the representational capacity is lower. Leveraging these properties, we propose a novel framework, Hyperbolic Feature Interpolation (HyFI), which interpolates between semantic and perceptual visual features along hyperbolic geodesics. This enables both the fusion and compression of perceptual and semantic information, effectively reflecting the limited expressiveness of brain signals and the entangled nature of these features. As a result, it facilitates better alignment between brain and visual features. We demonstrate that HyFI achieves state-of-the-art performance in zero-shot brain-to-image retrieval, outperforming prior methods with Top-1 accuracy improvements of up to +17.3% on THINGS-EEG and +9.1% on THINGS-MEG.

</details>


### [183] [Beyond Binary Correctness: Scaling Evaluation of Long-Horizon Agents on Subjective Enterprise Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22744)
*Abhishek Chandwani,Ishan Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LH-Bench：针对主观企业任务的三支柱评估框架，超越二元正确性，专注于自主长时程执行评估


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在数学、编程等客观可验证任务上表现出色，但现实企业工作通常是主观且依赖上下文的，成功取决于组织目标、用户意图以及长时程多工具工作流中产生的中间产物质量。现有评估方法无法有效评估这类主观任务。

Method: 提出LH-Bench三支柱评估设计：1) 专家基础评分标准，为LLM评委提供评估主观工作所需的领域上下文；2) 精心策划的真实产物，提供逐步奖励信号（如内容任务的章节级标注）；3) 成对人类偏好评估，用于收敛验证。

Result: 领域专家撰写的评分标准比LLM撰写的评分标准提供更可靠的评估信号（kappa = 0.60 vs. 0.46）。人类偏好判断确认了相同的顶级分离（p < 0.05），证明专家基础评估可以在不牺牲可靠性的情况下扩展。

Conclusion: LH-Bench框架能够有效评估自主长时程执行在主观企业任务上的表现，通过专家基础评估实现可扩展且可靠的评估，填补了当前LLM评估方法在主观任务领域的空白。

Abstract: Large language models excel on objectively verifiable tasks such as math and programming, where evaluation reduces to unit tests or a single correct answer. In contrast, real-world enterprise work is often subjective and context-dependent: success hinges on organizational goals, user intent, and the quality of intermediate artifacts produced across long, multi-tool workflows.
  We introduce LH-Bench, a three-pillar evaluation design that moves beyond binary correctness to score autonomous, long-horizon execution on subjective enterprise tasks. The pillars are: (i) expert-grounded rubrics that give LLM judges the domain context needed to score subjective work, (ii) curated ground-truth artifacts that enable stepwise reward signals (e.g., chapter-level annotation for content tasks), and (iii) pairwise human preference evaluation for convergent validation. We show that domain-authored rubrics provide substantially more reliable evaluation signals than LLM-authored rubrics (kappa = 0.60 vs. 0.46), and that human preference judgments confirm the same top-tier separation (p < 0.05), evidence that expert-grounded evaluation can scale without sacrificing reliability. We release public datasets and report results on two environments: Figma-to-code (33 real .fig tasks against the Figma API via MCP) and Programmatic content (41 courses comprising 183 individually-evaluated chapters on a course platform serving 30+ daily users).

</details>


### [184] [ABSTRAL: Automatic Design of Multi-Agent Systems Through Iterative Refinement and Topology Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22791)
*Weijia Song,Jiashu Yue,Zhe Pang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ABSTRAL框架将多智能体系统架构视为可演化的自然语言文档，通过对比轨迹分析进行精炼，实现了可检查、可修订、可转移的设计知识捕获。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
